### 6.10.2 StrategyEngineService（quantpf-strategy）

```java
@Slf4j
@Service
public class StrategyEngineService {

    private final Map<String, QuantStrategy> strategyMap;

    /** Spring 自动注入全部 QuantStrategy 实现，按名称建索引 */
    public StrategyEngineService(List<QuantStrategy> strategies) {
        this.strategyMap = strategies.stream()
                .collect(Collectors.toMap(QuantStrategy::getName, s -> s, (a, b) -> a));
    }

    /** 运行指定品种的全部策略，返回候选（按置信度降序） */
    public List<StrategyCandidate> runAll(StockDataDTO data, ProductType type) {
        if (data == null) return Collections.emptyList();
        return strategyMap.values().stream()
                .filter(s -> s.getProductType() == type)
                .map(s -> toCandidate(s, s.evaluate(data)))
                .filter(Objects::nonNull)
                .sorted(Comparator.comparingDouble(c -> -c.getConfidence()))
                .collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    /** 标的池盘后筛选：对单根日 K 运行指定品种的全部策略，返回命中结果列表（V18 细化） */
    public List<StrategyResult> runScreening(StockDataDTO data, ProductType type) {
        if (data == null) return Collections.emptyList();
        return strategyMap.values().stream()
                .filter(s -> s.getProductType() == type)
                .map(s -> s.evaluate(data))
                .filter(r -> r != null && !"HOLD".equals(r.getSignal()))
                .collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    /** 按策略编码获取策略实例（回测用） */
    public QuantStrategy getStrategy(String strategyName) {
        QuantStrategy s = strategyMap.get(strategyName);
        if (s == null) throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.STRATEGY_NOT_FOUND, "策略不存在: " + strategyName);
        return s;
    }

    private StrategyCandidate toCandidate(QuantStrategy strategy, StrategyResult r) {
        if (r == null || "HOLD".equals(r.getSignal())) return null;
        return StrategyCandidate.builder()
                .productType(r.getProductType())
                .symbol(r.getSymbol())
                .strategyName(strategy.getName())
                .signal(r.getSignal())
                .confidence(r.getConfidence() == null ? 0.0 : r.getConfidence())
                .entryPrice(r.getEntryPrice())
                .stopLoss(r.getStopLoss())
                .takeProfit(r.getTakeProfit())
                .reason(r.getReason())
                .build();
    }
}
```

### 6.10.3 RiskGuardService（quantpf-risk）

```java
@Slf4j
@Service
public class RiskGuardService {

    private static final List<String> RISK_KEYWORDS = List.of("加息", "监管", "制裁");
    private static final double DEFAULT_MAX_POSITION = 0.3;

    private final FuturesRiskRules futuresRiskRules;
    private final SpotRiskRules spotRiskRules;

    public RiskGuardService(FuturesRiskRules futuresRiskRules, SpotRiskRules spotRiskRules) {
        this.futuresRiskRules = futuresRiskRules;
        this.spotRiskRules = spotRiskRules;
    }

    /** 统一风控入口（确定性硬闸门）。期货/现货专属规则见 §6.4 签名，此处只做调用分发。 */
    public RiskResult checkRisk(MarketDataDTO marketData, ProductType type) {
        if (marketData == null) return RiskResult.block("市场数据为空", type);
        List<String> triggered = new ArrayList<>(checkCommonRisk(marketData));
        switch (type) {
            case FUTURE -> triggered.addAll(futuresRiskRules.checkAll(marketData));
            case SPOT -> triggered.addAll(spotRiskRules.checkAll(marketData));
            case STOCK -> { /* 股票无额外风控 */ }
        }
        return buildResult(triggered, type);
    }

    /** 下单前同步风控（强一致），通过才允许下单 */
    public RiskResult checkBeforeTrade(StrategyCandidate signal) {
        if (signal == null) return RiskResult.block("信号为空", ProductType.STOCK);
        List<String> triggered = new ArrayList<>();
        if (signal.getStopLoss() == null) triggered.add("缺少止损价");
        if (signal.getConfidence() != null && signal.getConfidence() < 0.5) triggered.add("置信度不足");
        return buildResult(triggered, signal.getProductType());
    }

    private RiskResult buildResult(List<String> triggered, ProductType type) {
        double maxPosition = triggered.isEmpty() ? DEFAULT_MAX_POSITION
                : (triggered.size() <= 1 ? 0.1 : 0.0);
        return RiskResult.builder()
                .allowTrading(triggered.isEmpty())
                .maxPosition(maxPosition)
                .reason(triggered.isEmpty() ? "正常" : "触发风控: " + String.join(", ", triggered))
                .productType(type)
                .build();
    }

    private List<String> checkCommonRisk(MarketDataDTO m) {
        List<String> triggered = new ArrayList<>();
        if (m.getIndexChange() != null && m.getIndexChange() < -0.03) triggered.add("大盘跌幅超3%");
        if (m.getVix() != null && m.getVix() > 30) triggered.add("VIX恐慌指数过高");
        if (m.getNews() != null && RISK_KEYWORDS.stream().anyMatch(m.getNews()::contains)) triggered.add("重大政策新闻");
        return triggered;
    }

    /** 紧急熔断：置熔断标记 + 写风控日志 + 告警 + 停止新订单 */
    public void emergencyHalt(String reason) {
        // 1. Redis 置 quant:risk:status = HALT
        // 2. 写 qn_risk_log(action=HALT)
        // 3. 发送告警（邮件/短信）
        log.error("紧急熔断触发: {}", reason);
    }
}
```

### 6.10.4 TradeExecutionService（quantpf-execution）

```java
@Slf4j
@Service
public class TradeExecutionService {

    private final Map<ExecutionRouteKey, BrokerAdapter> adapterMap;

    /** Spring 自动注入全部适配器，按账户类型+品种+经纪商+执行模式建索引；冲突直接启动失败。 */
    public TradeExecutionService(List<BrokerAdapter> adapters) {
        this.adapterMap = adapters.stream()
                .collect(Collectors.toMap(BrokerAdapter::getProductType, a -> a, (a, b) -> a));
    }

    /** 执行交易：风控硬闸门 + 仓位换算 + 下单 */
    public ExecutionResult executeTrade(StrategyCandidate signal, RiskResult riskResult, AccountInfoDTO account) {
        if (signal == null) return ExecutionResult.fail("信号为空");
        if (!riskResult.isAllowTrading()) return ExecutionResult.blocked(riskResult.getReason());
        if (account == null || account.getTotalBalance() == null) return ExecutionResult.fail("账户资金信息缺失");

        ExecutionRouteKey routeKey = new ExecutionRouteKey(account.getAccountType(), signal.getProductType(), account.getBrokerProvider(), account.getExecutionMode());
        BrokerAdapter adapter = adapterMap.get(routeKey);
        if (adapter == null) return ExecutionResult.fail("未配置执行路由 " + routeKey);

        if (signal.getEntryPrice() == null || signal.getEntryPrice().compareTo(BigDecimal.ZERO) <= 0) {
            return ExecutionResult.fail("入场价无效");
        }

        BigDecimal lotSize = signal.getProductType() == ProductType.STOCK ? BigDecimal.valueOf(100) : BigDecimal.ONE;
        BigDecimal quantity = calculateQuantity(signal.getConfidence() == null ? 0 : signal.getConfidence(),
                riskResult.getMaxPosition(), account.getTotalBalance(), signal.getEntryPrice(), lotSize);
        if (quantity.compareTo(BigDecimal.ZERO) <= 0) return ExecutionResult.fail("计算出的下单数量为 0");

        TradeOrder order = TradeOrder.builder()
                .productType(signal.getProductType())
                .symbol(signal.getSymbol())
                .side(signal.getSignal())
                .orderType("LIMIT")
                .price(signal.getEntryPrice())
                .quantity(quantity)
                .stopLoss(signal.getStopLoss())
                .takeProfit(signal.getTakeProfit())
                .build();

        try {
            return adapter.submitOrder(order);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("下单异常, symbol={}, side={}", order.getSymbol(), order.getSide(), e);
            return ExecutionResult.fail("下单异常: " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    /** 目标仓位比例 → 目标金额 → 可下单数量，按最小交易单位取整（V17 修正） */
    public BigDecimal calculateQuantity(double confidence, double maxPositionRatio,
                                        BigDecimal totalCapital, BigDecimal price, BigDecimal lotSize) {
        double positionRatio = Math.min(confidence * 0.5, maxPositionRatio);
        BigDecimal targetAmount = totalCapital.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(positionRatio));
        BigDecimal rawQty = targetAmount.divide(price, 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
        return rawQty.divide(lotSize, 0, RoundingMode.DOWN).multiply(lotSize);
    }
}
```

### 6.10.5 AiAnalysisClient（quantpf-common，共享）

```java
@Slf4j
@Service
public class AiAnalysisClient {

    private final RestClient restClient;
    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    private final LocalNewsRuleEngine localNewsRuleEngine;   // 本地降级（§6.4 签名）
    private final LocalFactorScorer localFactorScorer;       // 本地降级（§6.5 签名）

    @Value("${quant.ai.base-url}") private String baseUrl;
    @Value("${quant.ai.api-key}") private String apiKey;

    public AiAnalysisClient(RestClient.Builder builder, ObjectMapper objectMapper,
                            LocalNewsRuleEngine localNewsRuleEngine, LocalFactorScorer localFactorScorer) {
        this.restClient = builder.build();
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
        this.localNewsRuleEngine = localNewsRuleEngine;
        this.localFactorScorer = localFactorScorer;
    }

    /** 通用 chat completion（OpenAI 兼容；timeout 建议在 RestClient 构建时统一配置） */
    public String chatCompletion(String model, String systemPrompt, String userPrompt, int timeoutSeconds) {
        Map<String, Object> body = Map.of(
                "model", model,
                "messages", List.of(
                        Map.of("role", "system", "content", systemPrompt),
                        Map.of("role", "user", "content", userPrompt)),
                "temperature", 0.1,
                "response_format", Map.of("type", "json_object"));
        String resp = restClient.post()
                .uri(baseUrl + "/chat/completions")
                .header("Authorization", "Bearer " + apiKey)
                .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
                .body(body)
                .retrieve()
                .body(String.class);
        return extractContent(resp);
    }

    private String extractContent(String resp) {
        try {
            JsonNode root = objectMapper.readTree(resp);
            return root.path("choices").get(0).path("message").path("content").asText();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.AI_PARSE_ERROR, "AI 响应解析失败", e);
        }
    }

    /** 新闻情绪分析（带降级） */
    public NewsAnalysisResult analyzeNewsWithFallback(String content) {
        try {
            String prompt = "你是金融新闻分析师，输出 JSON: {\"sentiment\":-1到1,\"label\":\"...\",\"keywords\":[...]}";
            String resp = chatCompletion("deepseek-chat", prompt, content, 10);
            return objectMapper.readValue(resp, NewsAnalysisResult.class);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.warn("DeepSeek 新闻分析失败，降级本地规则: {}", e.getMessage());
            return localNewsRuleEngine.analyze(content);
        }
    }

    /** 财报解读（带降级） */
    public FinanceAnalysisResult analyzeFinanceWithFallback(FinanceContext context) {
        try {
            String resp = chatCompletion("deepseek-reasoner", FINANCE_SYSTEM_PROMPT, context.toPrompt(), 60);
            return objectMapper.readValue(resp, FinanceAnalysisResult.class);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.warn("DeepSeek 财报分析失败，降级本地因子评分: {}", e.getMessage());
            return localFactorScorer.score(context.getStatements());
        }
    }
}
```

### 6.10.6 BacktestEngineService + PerformanceAnalyzer（quantpf-strategy）

```java
@Slf4j
@Service
public class BacktestEngineService {

    private static final double DEFAULT_MAX_POSITION = 0.3;                 // 默认最大仓位比例
    private static final BigDecimal DEFAULT_MARGIN_RATE = BigDecimal.ONE;   // 做空保证金率（简化：全额，期货可按交易所配置）

    private final QsBacktestResultMapper resultMapper;
    private final QsBacktestTradeMapper tradeMapper;
    private final QsBacktestEquityMapper equityMapper;
    private final MarketDataService marketDataService;
    private final StrategyEngineService strategyEngineService;
    private final PerformanceAnalyzer performanceAnalyzer;
    private final ThreadPoolExecutor backtestExecutor;

    public BacktestEngineService(QsBacktestResultMapper resultMapper, QsBacktestTradeMapper tradeMapper,
                                 QsBacktestEquityMapper equityMapper, MarketDataService marketDataService,
                                 StrategyEngineService strategyEngineService, PerformanceAnalyzer performanceAnalyzer,
                                 @Qualifier("backtestExecutor") ThreadPoolExecutor backtestExecutor) {
        this.resultMapper = resultMapper;
        this.tradeMapper = tradeMapper;
        this.equityMapper = equityMapper;
        this.marketDataService = marketDataService;
        this.strategyEngineService = strategyEngineService;
        this.performanceAnalyzer = performanceAnalyzer;
        this.backtestExecutor = backtestExecutor;
    }

    /** 提交回测（异步），返回 backtestId */
    public String executeBacktest(BacktestRequestDTO request) {
        validateRequest(request);
        String backtestId = IdUtil.fastSimpleUUID();
        QsBacktestResult result = buildPendingResult(backtestId, request);
        resultMapper.insert(result);   // 只写一条 PENDING 并提交，回测在事务外执行
        backtestExecutor.execute(() -> {
            try {
                doBacktest(backtestId, request);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.error("回测异常, backtestId={}", backtestId, e);
                updateStatus(backtestId, BacktestStatus.FAILED, e.getMessage());
            }
        });
        return backtestId;
    }

    private void doBacktest(String backtestId, BacktestRequestDTO request) {
        updateStatus(backtestId, BacktestStatus.RUNNING, null);
        KlinePeriod period = KlinePeriod.of(request.getPeriod());
        ProductType type = StringUtils.hasText(request.getProductType())
                ? ProductType.of(request.getProductType()) : ProductType.STOCK;
        QuantStrategy strategy = strategyEngineService.getStrategy(request.getStrategyName());

        List<KlineDTO> klines = marketDataService.fetchKlineList(
                request.getSymbols(), type, period, request.getStartDate(), request.getEndDate());
        if (CollectionUtils.isEmpty(klines)) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.DATA_EMPTY, "未查询到历史K线数据");
        }

        BigDecimal commissionRate = defaultIfNull(request.getCommissionRate(), new BigDecimal("0.001"));
        BigDecimal slippageRate = defaultIfNull(request.getSlippageRate(), new BigDecimal("0.001"));
        BigDecimal marginRate = DEFAULT_MARGIN_RATE;   // 做空保证金率（简化：全额）

        BacktestAccount account = new BacktestAccount(request.getInitialCapital());
        List<QsBacktestTrade> trades = new ArrayList<>();
        List<QsBacktestEquity> equities = new ArrayList<>();
        List<PendingOrder> pending = new ArrayList<>();

        for (int i = 0; i < klines.size(); i++) {
            KlineDTO kline = klines.get(i);

            // 1. 上一根 K 线挂出的委托，在本根成交（bar+1，避免前视偏差）
            List<PendingOrder> filled = new ArrayList<>();
            for (PendingOrder order : pending) {
                if (fillEntry(backtestId, order, kline, account, trades, commissionRate, slippageRate, marginRate)) {
                    filled.add(order);
                }
            }
            pending.removeAll(filled);

            // 2. 检查持仓止盈止损（本根高低价触及则平仓）
            checkStopLossTakeProfit(backtestId, kline, account, trades, commissionRate, slippageRate);

            // 3. 用截至本根收盘的数据跑策略，生成信号 → 挂到下根成交
            StockDataDTO data = toStockData(klines, i, type);
            StrategyResult signal = strategy.evaluate(data);
            if (signal != null && isEntry(signal.getSignal())) {
                PendingOrder order = buildPendingOrder(signal, account);
                if (order != null) pending.add(order);
            }

            // 4. 记录本根收盘权益
            equities.add(buildEquity(backtestId, kline, account));
        }

        // 回测结束强制平仓
        forceCloseAll(backtestId, klines.get(klines.size() - 1), account, trades, commissionRate, slippageRate);

        BacktestPerformance perf = performanceAnalyzer.analyze(
                request.getInitialCapital(), equities, trades, period);
        saveResults(backtestId, trades, equities, perf);
        updateStatus(backtestId, BacktestStatus.COMPLETED, null);
        log.info("回测完成, backtestId={}, 交易数={}, 收益率={}", backtestId, trades.size(), perf.getReturnRate());
    }

    // ===== 以下为私有辅助方法 =====

    /** bar+1 成交：买入按下一根开盘价×(1+滑点)成交，卖出/做空按开盘价×(1-滑点)成交。
     *  做空需满足保证金要求（现金 ≥ 名义金额 × 保证金率）。 */
    private boolean fillEntry(String backtestId, PendingOrder order, KlineDTO kline, BacktestAccount account,
                              List<QsBacktestTrade> trades, BigDecimal commissionRate, BigDecimal slippageRate,
                              BigDecimal marginRate) {
        boolean isBuy = order.getSide().startsWith("BUY");
        BigDecimal fillPrice = kline.getOpen().multiply(
                isBuy ? BigDecimal.ONE.add(slippageRate) : BigDecimal.ONE.subtract(slippageRate));
        BigDecimal notional = fillPrice.multiply(order.getQuantity());   // 名义金额
        BigDecimal commission = notional.multiply(commissionRate);

        if (isBuy) {
            if (account.getCash().compareTo(notional.add(commission)) < 0) return false; // 资金不足
        } else {
            BigDecimal requiredMargin = notional.multiply(marginRate);   // 做空保证金要求
            if (account.getCash().compareTo(requiredMargin) < 0) return false;
        }

        PositionSide side = isBuy ? PositionSide.LONG : PositionSide.SHORT;
        int tradeNo = trades.size() + 1;
        account.getPositions().add(BacktestPosition.builder()
                .symbol(order.getSymbol()).side(side).quantity(order.getQuantity())
                .entryPrice(fillPrice).entryTime(kline.getTime())
                .takeProfit(order.getTakeProfit()).stopLoss(order.getStopLoss()).tradeNo(tradeNo).build());
        // 买入：现金 -= 名义金额 + 手续费；做空：现金 += 名义金额 - 手续费（买回负债由持仓市值负数体现）
        account.setCash(account.getCash().add(isBuy
                ? notional.negate().subtract(commission)
                : notional.subtract(commission)));

        trades.add(QsBacktestTrade.builder()
                .backtestId(backtestId).tradeNo(tradeNo)
                .symbol(order.getSymbol()).productType(ProductType.STOCK).side(order.getSide())
                .entryPrice(fillPrice).entryTime(kline.getTime())
                .quantity(order.getQuantity()).entryCommission(commission)
                .status("OPEN").entryReason(order.getReason()).build());
        return true;
    }

    /** 止盈止损：LONG 高触止盈/低触止损；SHORT 低触止盈/高触止损 */
    private void checkStopLossTakeProfit(String backtestId, KlineDTO kline, BacktestAccount account,
                                         List<QsBacktestTrade> trades, BigDecimal commissionRate, BigDecimal slippageRate) {
        List<BacktestPosition> closed = new ArrayList<>();
        for (BacktestPosition pos : account.getPositions()) {
            BigDecimal exitPrice = null; String exitReason = null;
            if (pos.getSide() == PositionSide.LONG) {
                if (pos.getTakeProfit() != null && kline.getHigh().compareTo(pos.getTakeProfit()) >= 0) { exitPrice = pos.getTakeProfit(); exitReason = "TAKE_PROFIT"; }
                else if (pos.getStopLoss() != null && kline.getLow().compareTo(pos.getStopLoss()) <= 0) { exitPrice = pos.getStopLoss(); exitReason = "STOP_LOSS"; }
            } else {
                if (pos.getTakeProfit() != null && kline.getLow().compareTo(pos.getTakeProfit()) <= 0) { exitPrice = pos.getTakeProfit(); exitReason = "TAKE_PROFIT"; }
                else if (pos.getStopLoss() != null && kline.getHigh().compareTo(pos.getStopLoss()) >= 0) { exitPrice = pos.getStopLoss(); exitReason = "STOP_LOSS"; }
            }
            if (exitPrice != null) {
                closePosition(backtestId, kline, pos, exitPrice, exitReason, account, trades, commissionRate, slippageRate);
                closed.add(pos);
            }
        }
        account.getPositions().removeAll(closed);
    }

    /** 平仓并结算盈亏，更新对应交易明细。
     *  现金：LONG 平仓 = 现金 + 卖出收入 - 手续费；SHORT 平仓 = 现金 - 买回支出 - 手续费。 */
    private void closePosition(String backtestId, KlineDTO kline, BacktestPosition pos, BigDecimal exitPrice,
                               String exitReason, BacktestAccount account, List<QsBacktestTrade> trades,
                               BigDecimal commissionRate, BigDecimal slippageRate) {
        BigDecimal amount = exitPrice.multiply(pos.getQuantity());
        BigDecimal exitCommission = amount.multiply(commissionRate);
        BigDecimal gross = pos.getSide() == PositionSide.LONG
                ? exitPrice.subtract(pos.getEntryPrice())
                : pos.getEntryPrice().subtract(exitPrice);

        account.setCash(account.getCash().add(pos.getSide() == PositionSide.LONG
                ? amount.subtract(exitCommission)
                : amount.negate().subtract(exitCommission)));

        for (QsBacktestTrade t : trades) {
            if (t.getTradeNo() == pos.getTradeNo() && "OPEN".equals(t.getStatus())) {
                BigDecimal entryCommission = t.getEntryCommission() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : t.getEntryCommission();
                BigDecimal profit = gross.multiply(pos.getQuantity())
                        .subtract(entryCommission).subtract(exitCommission);   // 含开/平仓手续费
                t.setExitPrice(exitPrice); t.setExitTime(kline.getTime());
                t.setExitCommission(exitCommission); t.setExitReason(exitReason);
                t.setProfit(profit); t.setStatus("CLOSED");
                t.setReturnRate(profit.divide(t.getEntryPrice().multiply(t.getQuantity()), 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP));
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    /** 回测结束强制平仓（按最后一根收盘价） */
    private void forceCloseAll(String backtestId, KlineDTO kline, BacktestAccount account,
                               List<QsBacktestTrade> trades, BigDecimal commissionRate, BigDecimal slippageRate) {
        for (BacktestPosition pos : new ArrayList<>(account.getPositions())) {
            closePosition(backtestId, kline, pos, kline.getClose(), "FORCE_CLOSE", account, trades, commissionRate, slippageRate);
        }
        account.getPositions().clear();
    }

    private StockDataDTO toStockData(List<KlineDTO> klines, int i, ProductType type) {
        KlineDTO cur = klines.get(i);
        return StockDataDTO.builder()
                .productType(type).symbol(cur.getSymbol()).time(cur.getTime())
                .close(cur.getClose()).volume(cur.getVolume())
                .klineSeries(new ArrayList<>(klines.subList(0, i + 1)))
                .build();
    }

    private boolean isEntry(String signal) {
        return "BUY".equals(signal) || "SELL".equals(signal)
                || "BUY_OPEN".equals(signal) || "SELL_OPEN".equals(signal);
    }

    /** 依据信号置信度 + 当前可用资金 + 入场价计算下单数量（消除占位，与 §6.10.4 逻辑一致） */
    private PendingOrder buildPendingOrder(StrategyResult signal, BacktestAccount account) {
        if (signal.getEntryPrice() == null || signal.getEntryPrice().compareTo(BigDecimal.ZERO) <= 0) return null;
        BigDecimal lotSize = signal.getProductType() == ProductType.STOCK ? BigDecimal.valueOf(100) : BigDecimal.ONE;
        BigDecimal quantity = calculateQuantity(
                signal.getConfidence() == null ? 0 : signal.getConfidence(),
                DEFAULT_MAX_POSITION, account.getCash(), signal.getEntryPrice(), lotSize);
        if (quantity.compareTo(BigDecimal.ZERO) <= 0) return null;
        return PendingOrder.builder()
                .symbol(signal.getSymbol()).side(signal.getSignal())
                .price(signal.getEntryPrice()).quantity(quantity)
                .takeProfit(signal.getTakeProfit()).stopLoss(signal.getStopLoss())
                .reason(signal.getReason()).build();
    }

    /** 目标仓位比例 → 目标金额 → 可下单数量，按最小交易单位取整（与 §6.10.4 TradeExecutionService 一致，建议抽到公共 PositionSizer） */
    private BigDecimal calculateQuantity(double confidence, double maxPositionRatio,
                                         BigDecimal totalCapital, BigDecimal price, BigDecimal lotSize) {
        double positionRatio = Math.min(confidence * 0.5, maxPositionRatio);
        BigDecimal targetAmount = totalCapital.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(positionRatio));
        BigDecimal rawQty = targetAmount.divide(price, 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
        return rawQty.divide(lotSize, 0, RoundingMode.DOWN).multiply(lotSize);
    }

    private QsBacktestEquity buildEquity(String backtestId, KlineDTO kline, BacktestAccount account) {
        return QsBacktestEquity.builder()
                .backtestId(backtestId).klineTime(kline.getTime())
                .totalEquity(account.totalEquity(kline)).cash(account.getCash())
                .positionValue(account.positionValue(kline)).positionCount(account.getPositions().size())
                .build();
    }

    private QsBacktestResult buildPendingResult(String backtestId, BacktestRequestDTO request) {
        return QsBacktestResult.builder()
                .backtestId(backtestId)
                .strategyName(request.getStrategyName()).strategyParams(request.getStrategyParams())
                .period(request.getPeriod()).productType(defaultIfBlank(request.getProductType(), "STOCK"))
                .symbols(request.getSymbols() == null ? null : JsonUtils.toJson(request.getSymbols()))
                .startDate(request.getStartDate()).endDate(request.getEndDate())
                .initialCapital(request.getInitialCapital())
                .commissionRate(defaultIfNull(request.getCommissionRate(), new BigDecimal("0.001")))
                .slippageRate(defaultIfNull(request.getSlippageRate(), new BigDecimal("0.001")))
                .adjustFlag(defaultIfBlank(request.getAdjustFlag(), "FORWARD"))
                .status(BacktestStatus.PENDING.name()).build();
    }

    private void validateRequest(BacktestRequestDTO request) {
        if (request == null || request.getInitialCapital() == null) throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.PARAM_INVALID, "回测请求为空");
        if (request.getStartDate().isAfter(request.getEndDate())) throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.PARAM_INVALID, "开始日期晚于结束日期");
    }

    private void updateStatus(String backtestId, BacktestStatus status, String errorMessage) {
        resultMapper.update(null, new LambdaUpdateWrapper<QsBacktestResult>()
                .eq(QsBacktestResult::getBacktestId, backtestId)
                .set(QsBacktestResult::getStatus, status.name())
                .set(QsBacktestResult::getErrorMessage, errorMessage)
                .set(status == BacktestStatus.COMPLETED, QsBacktestResult::getCompleteTime, LocalDateTime.now()));
    }

    private void saveResults(String backtestId, List<QsBacktestTrade> trades,
                             List<QsBacktestEquity> equities, BacktestPerformance perf) {
        QsBacktestResult result = resultMapper.selectOne(new LambdaQueryWrapper<QsBacktestResult>()
                .eq(QsBacktestResult::getBacktestId, backtestId));
        if (result == null) return;
        result.setFinalCapital(perf.getFinalCapital()); result.setTotalProfit(perf.getTotalProfit());
        result.setReturnRate(perf.getReturnRate()); result.setAnnualizedReturn(perf.getAnnualizedReturn());
        result.setMaxDrawdown(perf.getMaxDrawdown()); result.setWinRate(perf.getWinRate());
        result.setSharpeRatio(perf.getSharpeRatio()); result.setTotalTrades((int) perf.getTotalTrades());
        resultMapper.updateById(result);

        BatchInsertUtil.batchInsert(trades, 600, tradeMapper::insertBatch);
        BatchInsertUtil.batchInsert(equities, 600, equityMapper::insertBatch);
    }

    private BigDecimal defaultIfNull(BigDecimal v, BigDecimal def) { return v == null ? def : v; }
    private String defaultIfBlank(String v, String def) { return StringUtils.hasText(v) ? v : def; }
}

// ========== 绩效分析器 ==========

@Slf4j
@Component
public class PerformanceAnalyzer {

    private static final BigDecimal RISK_FREE_RATE = new BigDecimal("0.03");

    /** 计算收益/回撤/胜率/盈亏比/年化波动率/夏普（年化按 KlinePeriod.annualFactor 折算） */
    public BacktestPerformance analyze(BigDecimal initialCapital, List<QsBacktestEquity> equities,
                                       List<QsBacktestTrade> trades, KlinePeriod period) {
        if (CollectionUtils.isEmpty(equities)) {
            return BacktestPerformance.builder().initialCapital(initialCapital).finalCapital(initialCapital)
                    .totalProfit(BigDecimal.ZERO).returnRate(BigDecimal.ZERO).build();
        }

        BigDecimal finalCapital = equities.get(equities.size() - 1).getTotalEquity();
        BigDecimal totalProfit = finalCapital.subtract(initialCapital);
        BigDecimal returnRate = totalProfit.divide(initialCapital, 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);

        // 最大回撤 + 峰谷
        BigDecimal peak = initialCapital, maxDrawdown = BigDecimal.ZERO;
        BigDecimal peakCapital = initialCapital, troughCapital = initialCapital;
        for (QsBacktestEquity e : equities) {
            BigDecimal v = e.getTotalEquity();
            if (v.compareTo(peak) > 0) peak = v;
            else {
                BigDecimal dd = peak.subtract(v).divide(peak, 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
                if (dd.compareTo(maxDrawdown) > 0) maxDrawdown = dd;
            }
            if (v.compareTo(peakCapital) > 0) peakCapital = v;
            if (v.compareTo(troughCapital) < 0) troughCapital = v;
        }

        // 交易统计（仅已平仓）
        List<BigDecimal> profits = trades.stream()
                .filter(t -> "CLOSED".equals(t.getStatus()) && t.getProfit() != null)
                .map(QsBacktestTrade::getProfit).collect(Collectors.toList());
        long totalTrades = profits.size();
        long winTrades = profits.stream().filter(p -> p.signum() > 0).count();
        long lossTrades = profits.stream().filter(p -> p.signum() < 0).count();
        BigDecimal winRate = totalTrades > 0
                ? BigDecimal.valueOf(winTrades).divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(totalTrades), 4, RoundingMode.HALF_UP) : BigDecimal.ZERO;
        BigDecimal avgWin = avgOf(profits, p -> p.signum() > 0);
        BigDecimal avgLoss = avgOf(profits, p -> p.signum() < 0);
        BigDecimal profitLossRatio = avgLoss.signum() != 0 ? avgWin.divide(avgLoss.abs(), 2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP) : BigDecimal.ZERO;

        // 周期收益率序列 → 年化波动率 / 年化收益 / 夏普
        List<Double> returns = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 1; i < equities.size(); i++) {
            BigDecimal prev = equities.get(i - 1).getTotalEquity();
            BigDecimal curr = equities.get(i).getTotalEquity();
            if (prev.signum() != 0) returns.add(curr.subtract(prev).divide(prev, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).doubleValue());
        }
        double mean = returns.stream().mapToDouble(Double::doubleValue).average().orElse(0.0);
        double variance = returns.stream().mapToDouble(r -> (r - mean) * (r - mean)).sum() / Math.max(1, returns.size());
        double std = Math.sqrt(variance);
        int factor = period.annualFactor();
        double annualVol = std * Math.sqrt(factor);
        double annualReturn = returns.isEmpty() ? 0.0
                : Math.pow(1 + returnRate.doubleValue(), (double) factor / returns.size()) - 1;
        double sharpe = annualVol == 0 ? 0.0 : (annualReturn - RISK_FREE_RATE.doubleValue()) / annualVol;

        long days = ChronoUnit.DAYS.between(equities.get(0).getKlineTime().toLocalDate(),
                equities.get(equities.size() - 1).getKlineTime().toLocalDate());

        return BacktestPerformance.builder()
                .initialCapital(initialCapital).finalCapital(finalCapital)
                .totalProfit(totalProfit).returnRate(returnRate)
                .annualizedReturn(bd(annualReturn)).annualizedVolatility(bd(annualVol))
                .peakCapital(peakCapital).troughCapital(troughCapital).maxDrawdown(maxDrawdown)
                .totalTrades(totalTrades).winTrades(winTrades).lossTrades(lossTrades)
                .winRate(winRate).avgWin(avgWin).avgLoss(avgLoss).profitLossRatio(profitLossRatio)
                .sharpeRatio(bd(sharpe)).days(days)
                .build();
    }

    private BigDecimal avgOf(List<BigDecimal> values, java.util.function.Predicate<BigDecimal> predicate) {
        List<BigDecimal> filtered = values.stream().filter(predicate).collect(Collectors.toList());
        if (filtered.isEmpty()) return BigDecimal.ZERO;
        return filtered.stream().reduce(BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal::add)
                .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(filtered.size()), 2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
    }
    private BigDecimal bd(double v) { return BigDecimal.valueOf(v).setScale(6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP); }
}
```

> **落地提示**：`backtestExecutor` 为 `quantpf-common.config` 中第二个线程池 Bean（core 2 / max 4 / queue 100）；`MarketDataService.fetchKlineList(symbols, type, period, start, end)` 为 V18 新增的批量 K 线查询方法（§6.2 中 `fetchKline` 的批量版）。

---

## 6.11 交易日历模块（quantpf-common，跨模块基础服务）

> **定位**：交易日历是风控、交易主循环、标的池、回测等所有"是否可交易 / 哪天是交易日"判断的基础。V21 新增该模块，提供交易日判断、交易时段判断、日期推算与日历导入能力。
>
> **依赖**：交易日历为跨模块基础能力，置于 `quantpf-common.calendar`，实体/Mapper 在 `quantpf-common`，供 risk / execution / strategy / data 共用，避免重复依赖。

### 6.11.1 表设计（DDL 见 §8.13）

- `qm_trading_calendar`：交易日历（按交易所 × 日期），`is_trading_day` 标记是否交易日。
- `qm_trading_session`：交易时段（按交易所 × 品种 × 时段序号），支持早盘 / 午盘 / 夜盘，夜盘可跨日。

### 6.11.2 核心类（完整实现）

```java
// ===== 交易日历服务 =====
@Slf4j
@Service
public class TradingCalendarService {

    private final QmTradingCalendarMapper calendarMapper;
    private final QmTradingSessionMapper sessionMapper;

    public TradingCalendarService(QmTradingCalendarMapper calendarMapper, QmTradingSessionMapper sessionMapper) {
        this.calendarMapper = calendarMapper;
        this.sessionMapper = sessionMapper;
    }

    /** 判断某日是否交易日（日历缺失时保守返回 false） */
    public boolean isTradingDay(LocalDate date, String exchange) {
        QmTradingCalendar c = calendarMapper.selectOne(new LambdaQueryWrapper<QmTradingCalendar>()
                .eq(QmTradingCalendar::getTradeDate, date)
                .eq(QmTradingCalendar::getExchange, exchange));
        return c != null && c.getIsTradingDay() != null && c.getIsTradingDay() == 1;
    }

    /** 判断当前是否处于交易时段（含交易日判断） */
    public boolean isTradingTimeNow(ProductType type) {
        String exchange = resolveExchange(type);
        if (!isTradingDay(LocalDate.now(), exchange)) return false;
        LocalTime now = LocalTime.now();
        return getSessions(type, exchange).stream().anyMatch(s -> inSession(now, s));
    }

    /** 下一交易日（最多向后探测 30 天） */
    public LocalDate nextTradingDay(LocalDate date, String exchange) {
        LocalDate d = date.plusDays(1);
        int guard = 0;
        while (guard++ < 30 && !isTradingDay(d, exchange)) d = d.plusDays(1);
        return d;
    }

    /** 上一交易日 */
    public LocalDate previousTradingDay(LocalDate date, String exchange) {
        LocalDate d = date.minusDays(1);
        int guard = 0;
        while (guard++ < 30 && !isTradingDay(d, exchange)) d = d.minusDays(1);
        return d;
    }

    /** 最近 n 个交易日（含 endDate，升序返回），供回测/标的池取交易日序列 */
    public List<LocalDate> recentTradingDays(LocalDate endDate, int n, String exchange) {
        List<LocalDate> result = new ArrayList<>();
        LocalDate d = endDate;
        while (result.size() < n) {
            if (isTradingDay(d, exchange)) result.add(0, d);
            d = d.minusDays(1);
        }
        return result;
    }

    /** 某品种在指定交易所的交易时段 */
    public List<QmTradingSession> getSessions(ProductType type, String exchange) {
        return sessionMapper.selectList(new LambdaQueryWrapper<QmTradingSession>()
                .eq(QmTradingSession::getExchange, exchange)
                .eq(QmTradingSession::getProductType, type.getCode())
                .orderByAsc(QmTradingSession::getSortOrder));
    }

    private boolean inSession(LocalTime now, QmTradingSession s) {
        LocalTime start = s.getStartTime();
        LocalTime end = s.getEndTime();
        if (s.getIsNight() != null && s.getIsNight() == 1 && start.isAfter(end)) {
            // 跨日时段（如夜盘 21:00 - 次日 02:30）
            return !now.isBefore(start) || !now.isAfter(end);
        }
        return !now.isBefore(start) && !now.isAfter(end);
    }

    private String resolveExchange(ProductType type) {
        return switch (type) {
            case FUTURE -> "CFFEX";   // 默认中金所，具体品种由 session 表按交易所配置
            case SPOT -> "SPOT";
            default -> "SSE";
        };
    }
}

// ===== 交易日历导入 =====
@Slf4j
@Service
public class TradingCalendarLoader {

    private final QmTradingCalendarMapper calendarMapper;
    private final RestTemplate restTemplate;

    @Value("${quant.calendar.source:tushare}") private String source;
    @Value("${TUSHARE_TOKEN}") private String token;

    /** 从 Tushare trade_cal 导入指定年度日历（upsert 语义） */
    public int importYear(int year) {
        Map<String, Object> params = Map.of(
                "api_name", "trade_cal",
                "token", token,
                "params", Map.of("exchange", "SSE",
                        "start_date", year + "0101", "end_date", year + "1231"),
                "fields", "exchange,cal_date,is_open");
        TushareResponse resp = restTemplate.postForObject("https://api.tushare.pro", params, TushareResponse.class);
        if (resp == null || resp.getData() == null) return 0;

        int cnt = 0;
        for (Map<String, Object> row : resp.getData().getItems()) {
            QmTradingCalendar entity = QmTradingCalendar.builder()
                    .exchange((String) row.get("exchange"))
                    .tradeDate(LocalDate.parse((String) row.get("cal_date")))
                    .isTradingDay("1".equals(String.valueOf(row.get("is_open"))) ? 1 : 0)
                    .isWeekend(0)
                    .build();
            int updated = calendarMapper.update(entity, new LambdaUpdateWrapper<QmTradingCalendar>()
                    .eq(QmTradingCalendar::getExchange, entity.getExchange())
                    .eq(QmTradingCalendar::getTradeDate, entity.getTradeDate()));
            if (updated == 0) calendarMapper.insert(entity);
            cnt++;
        }
        log.info("交易日历导入完成, year={}, count={}", year, cnt);
        return cnt;
    }

    /** 导入下一年度（覆盖回测/调度所需的未来日历） */
    public int importNextYear() {
        return importYear(LocalDate.now().getYear() + 1);
    }
}
```

> **数据源说明**：交易日历优先用 Tushare `trade_cal`（含 `is_open` 字段），降级为手动 CSV/Excel 导入。交易日历需在每年初导入下一年度，否则年末回测/调度会缺未来日期。

### 6.11.3 异常与边界

- 日历缺失时 `isTradingDay` 默认返回 `false`（保守：视为非交易日，不触发交易）。
- 夜盘跨日时段需用 `start > end` 判断跨日；实际落地时还需处理"夜盘属于下一交易日"的归属问题（V21 简化：归属当前自然日）。

---

## 6.12 模拟盘撮合引擎（quantpf-execution）

> **定位**：模拟盘是 MVP 的核心闭环——在无真实券商接入时，用模拟撮合验证"策略 → 风控 → 执行 → 成交 → 持仓 → 账户"全链路。`PaperBrokerAdapter` 实现 `BrokerAdapter` 接口，使 `TradeExecutionService` 无需改动即可路由到模拟撮合。
>
> **与回测的区别**：回测是"事后批量重放历史"；模拟盘是"实时逐笔撮合"，用最新行情驱动，走真实订单 / 持仓 / 成交 / 账户表（`account_type=VIRTUAL`）。

### 6.12.1 撮合规则（MVP 简化，可逐步增强）

| 规则 | 说明 |
|:---|:---|
| 限价单 LIMIT | 买：最新价 ≤ 委托价成交；卖：最新价 ≥ 委托价成交。成交价 = 委托价（保守，与回测"触及价成交"一致） |
| 市价单 MARKET | 按最新价立即成交 |
| 涨跌停约束 | A 股默认 ±10%（涨停 = 昨收×1.1，跌停 = 昨收×0.9）；一字涨停无法买入、一字跌停无法卖出 |
| T+1 | A 股当日买入的持仓当日不可卖（`available_quantity=0`），次日由 `paperDailyResetJob` 释放 |
| 手续费 | 佣金（默认万 2.5，最低 5 元）+ 卖出印花税（万 5）+ 过户费（可配置） |
| 资金/持仓校验 | 买入校验可用现金；卖出校验可卖数量；做空校验保证金 |
| 部分成交 | MVP 不模拟部分成交（要么全成要么不成），进阶可加 |

### 6.12.2 表设计（DDL 见 §8.13）

- `qt_account`：账户资金表（虚拟/真实共用），记录现金 / 冻结 / 保证金 / 市值 / 总资产 / 盈亏。
- 复用 `qt_trade_order`（`account_type=VIRTUAL`）、`qt_trade_record`、`qt_position`。

### 6.12.3 核心类（完整实现）

```java
// ===== 行情快照（撮合输入，由 MarketDataService.getSnapshot 提供） =====
@Data
@Builder
public class QuoteSnapshot {
    private String symbol;
    private BigDecimal lastPrice;   // 最新价
    private BigDecimal prevClose;   // 昨收（用于涨跌停）
    private BigDecimal highLimit;   // 涨停价（可选，null 则不校验）
    private BigDecimal lowLimit;    // 跌停价（可选）
}

// ===== 虚拟账户服务 =====
@Slf4j
@Service
public class PaperAccountService {

    private final QtAccountMapper accountMapper;

    public PaperAccountService(QtAccountMapper accountMapper) {
        this.accountMapper = accountMapper;
    }

    /** 初始化虚拟账户 */
    public QtAccount initAccount(String accountId, String accountName, BigDecimal initialCapital) {
        if (accountMapper.selectOne(new LambdaQueryWrapper<QtAccount>()
                .eq(QtAccount::getAccountId, accountId)) != null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.PARAM_INVALID, "账户已存在: " + accountId);
        }
        QtAccount acc = QtAccount.builder()
                .accountId(accountId).accountType(AccountType.VIRTUAL.getCode())
                .accountName(accountName).initialCapital(initialCapital)
                .cash(initialCapital).frozenCash(BigDecimal.ZERO)
                .marginUsed(BigDecimal.ZERO).marketValue(BigDecimal.ZERO)
                .totalAsset(initialCapital).realizedPnl(BigDecimal.ZERO).unrealizedPnl(BigDecimal.ZERO)
                .status("ACTIVE").build();
        accountMapper.insert(acc);
        return acc;
    }

    /** 查询账户 */
    public QtAccount getAccount(String accountId) {
        QtAccount acc = accountMapper.selectOne(new LambdaQueryWrapper<QtAccount>()
                .eq(QtAccount::getAccountId, accountId));
        if (acc == null) throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.PARAM_INVALID, "账户不存在: " + accountId);
        return acc;
    }

    /** 更新账户 */
    public void updateAccount(QtAccount acc) {
        accountMapper.updateById(acc);
    }

    /** 冻结资金（挂单占用，MVP 快照撮合可不使用，保留用于进阶挂单撮合） */
    public void freezeCash(String accountId, BigDecimal amount) {
        QtAccount acc = getAccount(accountId);
        if (acc.getCash().compareTo(amount) < 0) throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.PARAM_INVALID, "可用资金不足");
        acc.setCash(acc.getCash().subtract(amount));
        acc.setFrozenCash(acc.getFrozenCash().add(amount));
        accountMapper.updateById(acc);
    }

    /** 解冻资金 */
    public void unfreezeCash(String accountId, BigDecimal amount) {
        QtAccount acc = getAccount(accountId);
        acc.setFrozenCash(acc.getFrozenCash().subtract(amount));
        acc.setCash(acc.getCash().add(amount));
        accountMapper.updateById(acc);
    }

    /** 重算账户市值 / 总资产 / 浮动盈亏 */
    public void refreshAsset(String accountId, BigDecimal marketValue) {
        QtAccount acc = getAccount(accountId);
        acc.setMarketValue(marketValue);
        acc.setTotalAsset(acc.getCash().add(acc.getFrozenCash()).add(marketValue));
        acc.setUnrealizedPnl(acc.getTotalAsset().subtract(acc.getInitialCapital()).subtract(acc.getRealizedPnl()));
        accountMapper.updateById(acc);
    }
}

// ===== 模拟撮合引擎 =====
@Slf4j
@Service
public class PaperMatchingEngine {

    private final PaperAccountService accountService;
    private final QtPositionMapper positionMapper;
    private final QtTradeRecordMapper recordMapper;
    private final MarketDataService marketDataService;

    @Value("${quant.paper.fee.commission-rate:0.00025}") private BigDecimal commissionRate;   // 佣金
    @Value("${quant.paper.fee.min-commission:5}") private BigDecimal minCommission;          // 最低佣金
    @Value("${quant.paper.fee.stamp-tax-rate:0.0005}") private BigDecimal stampTaxRate;      // 印花税（卖出）
    @Value("${quant.paper.t-plus-1:true}") private boolean tPlus1;

    public PaperMatchingEngine(PaperAccountService accountService, QtPositionMapper positionMapper,
                               QtTradeRecordMapper recordMapper, MarketDataService marketDataService) {
        this.accountService = accountService;
        this.positionMapper = positionMapper;
        this.recordMapper = recordMapper;
        this.marketDataService = marketDataService;
    }

    /** 撮合入口：返回执行结果，成交则落成交记录 / 持仓 / 账户 */
    public ExecutionResult match(TradeOrder order) {
        String accountId = StringUtils.hasText(order.getAccountId()) ? order.getAccountId() : "PAPER-DEFAULT";
        QuoteSnapshot quote = marketDataService.getSnapshot(order.getSymbol());
        if (quote == null || quote.getLastPrice() == null) return ExecutionResult.fail("行情缺失，无法撮合");

        boolean isBuy = order.getSide().startsWith("BUY");
        BigDecimal fillPrice = resolveFillPrice(order, quote, isBuy);
        if (fillPrice == null) return ExecutionResult.fail("未成交（价格未触及或涨跌停）");

        BigDecimal amount = fillPrice.multiply(order.getQuantity());
        BigDecimal commission = calcCommission(amount);
        BigDecimal stampTax = isBuy ? BigDecimal.ZERO : amount.multiply(stampTaxRate);

        if (isBuy) {
            QtAccount acc = accountService.getAccount(accountId);
            if (acc.getCash().compareTo(amount.add(commission)) < 0) return ExecutionResult.fail("可用资金不足");
            acc.setCash(acc.getCash().subtract(amount).subtract(commission));
            accountService.updateAccount(acc);
            // T+1：当日买入可用量为 0，次日由 paperDailyResetJob 释放
            upsertPosition(accountId, order.getSymbol(), order.getQuantity(), tPlus1 ? 0 : order.getQuantity().longValue(), fillPrice);
        } else {
            QtPosition pos = getPosition(accountId, order.getSymbol());
            long sellable = pos == null ? 0 : pos.getAvailableQuantity();
            if (sellable < order.getQuantity().longValue()) return ExecutionResult.fail("可卖数量不足（含 T+1 限制）");
            reducePosition(accountId, order.getSymbol(), order.getQuantity().longValue());
            QtAccount acc = accountService.getAccount(accountId);
            acc.setCash(acc.getCash().add(amount).subtract(commission).subtract(stampTax));
            accountService.updateAccount(acc);
        }

        QtTradeRecord record = QtTradeRecord.builder()
                .tradeNo(IdUtil.fastSimpleUUID()).orderNo(order.getOrderNo())
                .productType(order.getProductType().getCode()).symbol(order.getSymbol())
                .side(order.getSide()).price(fillPrice).quantity(order.getQuantity().longValue())
                .amount(amount).commission(commission).stampTax(stampTax)
                .totalCost(commission.add(stampTax)).fillTime(LocalDateTime.now())
                .build();
        recordMapper.insert(record);
        log.info("模拟撮合成交, symbol={}, side={}, price={}, qty={}", order.getSymbol(), order.getSide(), fillPrice, order.getQuantity());
        return ExecutionResult.success(record.getTradeNo());
    }

    private BigDecimal resolveFillPrice(TradeOrder order, QuoteSnapshot quote, boolean isBuy) {
        if ("MARKET".equals(order.getOrderType())) {
            return checkLimit(quote.getLastPrice(), quote, isBuy) ? quote.getLastPrice() : null;
        }
        BigDecimal price = order.getPrice();
        if (price == null) return null;
        boolean touched = isBuy ? quote.getLastPrice().compareTo(price) <= 0 : quote.getLastPrice().compareTo(price) >= 0;
        if (!touched) return null;
        return checkLimit(price, quote, isBuy) ? price : null;
    }

    private boolean checkLimit(BigDecimal price, QuoteSnapshot quote, boolean isBuy) {
        if (quote.getHighLimit() != null && isBuy && price.compareTo(quote.getHighLimit()) > 0) return false;
        if (quote.getLowLimit() != null && !isBuy && price.compareTo(quote.getLowLimit()) < 0) return false;
        return true;
    }

    private BigDecimal calcCommission(BigDecimal amount) {
        return amount.multiply(commissionRate).max(minCommission);
    }

    /** 买入：新增或合并持仓（可用量受 T+1 影响），加权平均成本 */
    private void upsertPosition(String accountId, String symbol, BigDecimal quantity, long available, BigDecimal price) {
        QtPosition pos = getPosition(accountId, symbol);
        if (pos == null) {
            QtPosition p = QtPosition.builder()
                    .accountId(accountId).accountType(AccountType.VIRTUAL.getCode())
                    .productType(ProductType.STOCK.getCode()).symbol(symbol)
                    .positionSide(PositionSide.LONG.getCode())
                    .quantity(quantity.longValue()).availableQuantity(available).frozenQuantity(0L)
                    .avgEntryPrice(price).build();
            positionMapper.insert(p);
        } else {
            long newQty = pos.getQuantity() + quantity.longValue();
            BigDecimal newAvg = pos.getAvgEntryPrice().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(pos.getQuantity()))
                    .add(price.multiply(quantity)).divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(newQty), 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
            pos.setQuantity(newQty);
            pos.setAvailableQuantity(pos.getAvailableQuantity() + available);
            pos.setAvgEntryPrice(newAvg);
            positionMapper.updateById(pos);
        }
    }

    /** 卖出：扣减持仓，减到 0 则删除 */
    private void reducePosition(String accountId, String symbol, long qty) {
        QtPosition pos = getPosition(accountId, symbol);
        if (pos == null || pos.getQuantity() < qty) throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.PARAM_INVALID, "持仓不足");
        long remain = pos.getQuantity() - qty;
        if (remain <= 0) {
            positionMapper.deleteById(pos.getPkId());
        } else {
            pos.setQuantity(remain);
            pos.setAvailableQuantity(Math.max(0, pos.getAvailableQuantity() - qty));
            positionMapper.updateById(pos);
        }
    }

    private QtPosition getPosition(String accountId, String symbol) {
        return positionMapper.selectOne(new LambdaQueryWrapper<QtPosition>()
                .eq(QtPosition::getAccountId, accountId)
                .eq(QtPosition::getAccountType, AccountType.VIRTUAL.getCode())
                .eq(QtPosition::getSymbol, symbol)
                .eq(QtPosition::getPositionSide, PositionSide.LONG.getCode()));
    }
}

// ===== 模拟券商适配器 =====
@Component
public class PaperBrokerAdapter implements BrokerAdapter {

    private static final String DEFAULT_ACCOUNT_ID = "PAPER-DEFAULT";

    private final PaperMatchingEngine matchingEngine;
    private final PaperAccountService accountService;
    private final QtPositionMapper positionMapper;

    public PaperBrokerAdapter(PaperMatchingEngine matchingEngine, PaperAccountService accountService, QtPositionMapper positionMapper) {
        this.matchingEngine = matchingEngine;
        this.accountService = accountService;
        this.positionMapper = positionMapper;
    }

    @Override
    public ProductType getProductType() { return ProductType.STOCK; }   // MVP 先支持股票

    @Override
    public ExecutionResult submitOrder(TradeOrder order) {
        return matchingEngine.match(order);
    }

    @Override
    public void cancelOrder(String orderId) {
        // MVP 快照撮合无挂单，撤单由订单服务将 qt_trade_order 置为 CANCELLED
        log.warn("模拟盘撤单（快照撮合无挂单）: {}", orderId);
    }

    @Override
    public List<PositionDTO> queryPositions(String accountId) {
        String acc = StringUtils.hasText(accountId) ? accountId : DEFAULT_ACCOUNT_ID;
        return positionMapper.selectList(new LambdaQueryWrapper<QtPosition>()
                .eq(QtPosition::getAccountId, acc)
                .eq(QtPosition::getAccountType, AccountType.VIRTUAL.getCode()))
                .stream().map(this::toPositionDTO).collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    @Override
    public AccountInfoDTO queryAccountInfo(String accountId) {
        String acc = StringUtils.hasText(accountId) ? accountId : DEFAULT_ACCOUNT_ID;
        QtAccount a = accountService.getAccount(acc);
        return AccountInfoDTO.builder()
                .accountId(a.getAccountId()).totalBalance(a.getTotalAsset())
                .availableBalance(a.getCash()).marginUsed(a.getMarginUsed()).build();
    }

    private PositionDTO toPositionDTO(QtPosition p) {
        return PositionDTO.builder()
                .symbol(p.getSymbol()).productType(ProductType.STOCK)
                .side(p.getPositionSide()).quantity(BigDecimal.valueOf(p.getQuantity()))
                .availableQuantity(BigDecimal.valueOf(p.getAvailableQuantity()))
                .avgEntryPrice(p.getAvgEntryPrice()).build();
    }
}
```

> **落地提示**：
> ① `PositionDTO` 为持仓共享 DTO（quantpf-common.dto），字段含 `symbol / productType / side / quantity / availableQuantity / avgEntryPrice`。
> ② `MarketDataService.getSnapshot(symbol)` 为 V21 新增方法：从 Redis 最新 Tick / K 线取 `QuoteSnapshot`（含昨收、涨跌停）。
> ③ `paperDailyResetJob` 在交易日开盘前将 VIRTUAL 持仓的 `available_quantity` 置为 `quantity`（释放 T+1），并重算账户资产。
> ④ `PaperBrokerAdapter` 与 `QmtBrokerAdapter` / `CtpBrokerAdapter` / `SpotBrokerAdapter` 一样实现 `BrokerAdapter`，Spring 自动注入后，`TradeExecutionService` 的 `adapterMap` 会包含 `STOCK→PaperBrokerAdapter`（与 QMT 同 key 时按优先级处理，MVP 默认只注册 Paper）。

---

## 6.13 数据源选型与获取方案（V23 新增）

> **目标**：为落地代码仓确定各数据类型的**免费可靠**来源与获取方式。核心原则：日线/日历可完全免费；分钟 K 免费但历史深度有限；盘口无免费历史、只能实时采集。

### 6.13.1 交易日历

| 来源 | 接口 | 费用 | 可靠性 | 字段 |
|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **Tushare Pro**（主） | `trade_cal` | 免费（基础积分） | ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ | `exchange` / `cal_date` / `is_open` / `pretrade_date` |
| AKShare（备） | `tool_trade_date_hist_sina()` | 免费 | ⭐⭐⭐⭐ | 日期列表 |
| 交易所官网 | 年度休市公告 | 免费 | ⭐⭐⭐ | 需手动解析 |

> 落地：主用 Tushare `trade_cal`，`is_open=1` 为交易日，按 `(exchange, trade_date)` upsert 到 `qm_trading_calendar`（§6.11 已实现 `TradingCalendarLoader`）。

### 6.13.2 K 线数据（日线 / 小时 / 分钟）

| 周期 | 免费可靠方案 | 说明 |
|:---|:---|:---|
| **日线** | Tushare `daily`（免费）+ `adj_factor`（复权因子，免费） | 最可靠，需自算复权 |
| | AKShare `stock_zh_a_hist`（东财，免费） | 免积分兜底 |
| **小时/分钟** | AKShare `stock_zh_a_minute`（东财/新浪，免费） | 免费但**历史深度有限** |
| | 东方财富 `datacenter-web.eastmoney.com` 免费 HTTP | 分钟 K 免费，需逆向参数 |
| | Tushare `stk_mins` | ⚠️ 需 5000 积分专业版（500 元/年） |

**复权**：前复权 = 收盘价 ×（当日 `adj_factor` / 最新 `adj_factor`），写入 `qs_kline_bar.adjust_flag=FORWARD`。

### 6.13.3 盘口数据（关键结论）

| 数据 | 免费？ | 说明 |
|:---|:---|:---|
| 实时五档盘口 | ✅ | 东财 `push2.eastmoney.com` WebSocket、新浪、腾讯推送 |
| 历史五档盘口 | ❌ | Level-2 行情需付费；Tushare/AKShare 均不提供 |

> 落地：盘口只能**实时订阅 → 按快照周期（1~3 秒）落库**到 `qs_order_book`（时序库），无法事后补历史。**爆点/缠论均为 K 线策略，不依赖五档盘口，MVP 可跳过盘口**，模拟撮合只用"最新价 + 昨收"（`QuoteSnapshot`）。

### 6.13.4 财务 / 估值数据

| 数据 | 免费来源 | 说明 |
|:---|:---|:---|
| 三大报表 | Tushare（2000 积分基础版约 200 元/年）；AKShare 免费 | 财报低频高价值 |
| PE/PB/市值 | Tushare `daily_basic`（低积分）或 AKShare | `pe_ttm`/`pb` 可直接取 |

---

## 6.14 策略算法实现：爆点 + 缠论（V23 新增）

> **定位**：V23 给出爆点策略的**完整 Java 实现**、缠论策略的**分型/笔/中枢骨架**（买卖点后期补充），以及**动态多策略管理**机制（策略表 `enabled` 状态列 + "停用才能修改"规则）。

### 6.14.1 动态策略管理机制

**规则：**

| 项 | 说明 |
|:---|:---|
| 启用过滤 | `StrategyEngineService` 只加载 `qs_strategy_config.enabled=1` 的策略 |
| 停用才能修改 | 修改策略参数前必须先停用；启用状态下禁止修改（或修改后强制重新启用） |
| 参数版本化 | 参数走 `qs_strategy_param_config` 多版本，`is_active=1` 为激活版，可回退 |
| 新增策略 | 写一个 `@Component implements QuantStrategy` 即完成注册，无需改代码/重启 |

**参数服务（读取激活版本参数）：**

```java
@Service
public class StrategyParamService {

    private final QsStrategyParamConfigMapper paramConfigMapper;

    public StrategyParamService(QsStrategyParamConfigMapper paramConfigMapper) {
        this.paramConfigMapper = paramConfigMapper;
    }

    /** 加载策略激活版本参数，反序列化为指定类型（param_values 为 JSON） */
    public <T> T loadParam(String strategyCode, Class<T> clazz) {
        QsStrategyParamConfig cfg = paramConfigMapper.selectOne(new LambdaQueryWrapper<QsStrategyParamConfig>()
                .eq(QsStrategyParamConfig::getStrategyCode, strategyCode)
                .eq(QsStrategyParamConfig::getIsActive, 1));
        if (cfg == null || cfg.getParamValues() == null) {
            return JsonUtils.fromJson("{}", clazz);   // 无激活版本时用默认值
        }
        return JsonUtils.fromJson(cfg.getParamValues(), clazz);
    }
}
```

**技术指标工具（爆点/缠论共用）：**

```java
public final class TechnicalIndicator {

    /** 简单移动平均（最近 n 根收盘价，含当前） */
    public static BigDecimal ma(List<KlineDTO> ks, int n) {
        if (ks == null || ks.isEmpty()) return BigDecimal.ZERO;
        int from = Math.max(0, ks.size() - n);
        BigDecimal sum = BigDecimal.ZERO; int cnt = 0;
        for (int i = from; i < ks.size(); i++) { sum = sum.add(ks.get(i).getClose()); cnt++; }
        return cnt == 0 ? BigDecimal.ZERO : sum.divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(cnt), 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
    }

    /** 最近 n 根平均成交量（含当前） */
    public static double avgVolume(List<KlineDTO> ks, int n) {
        if (ks == null || ks.isEmpty()) return 0;
        int from = Math.max(0, ks.size() - n);
        long sum = 0; int cnt = 0;
        for (int i = from; i < ks.size(); i++) { sum += ks.get(i).getVolume(); cnt++; }
        return cnt == 0 ? 0 : (double) sum / cnt;
    }

    /** 前 n 根最高价的最大值（不含当前 K 线，用于突破阻力位） */
    public static BigDecimal resistanceHigh(List<KlineDTO> ks, int n) {
        if (ks == null || ks.size() < 2) return null;
        int from = Math.max(0, ks.size() - 1 - n);
        BigDecimal max = null;
        for (int i = from; i < ks.size() - 1; i++) {
            if (max == null || ks.get(i).getHigh().compareTo(max) > 0) max = ks.get(i).getHigh();
        }
        return max;
    }

    /** ATR（平均真实波幅，简化均值，n 默认 14） */
    public static BigDecimal atr(List<KlineDTO> ks, int n) {
        if (ks == null || ks.size() < 2) return BigDecimal.ZERO;
        int from = Math.max(1, ks.size() - n);
        BigDecimal sum = BigDecimal.ZERO; int cnt = 0;
        for (int i = from; i < ks.size(); i++) {
            KlineDTO cur = ks.get(i), prev = ks.get(i - 1);
            BigDecimal tr = cur.getHigh().subtract(cur.getLow())
                    .max(cur.getHigh().subtract(prev.getClose()).abs())
                    .max(cur.getLow().subtract(prev.getClose()).abs());
            sum = sum.add(tr); cnt++;
        }
        return cnt == 0 ? BigDecimal.ZERO : sum.divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(cnt), 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
    }
}
```

### 6.14.2 爆点策略（完整实现）

**参数（存入 `qs_strategy_param_config.param_values`）：**

```json
{
  "breakoutPeriod": 20,
  "volumeMaPeriod": 5,
  "volumeRatio": 2.0,
  "breakoutMinPct": 0.01,
  "stopMode": "KLINE_LOW",
  "atrPeriod": 14,
  "atrMultiple": 2.0,
  "takeProfitRatio": 2.0,
  "maxHoldingDays": 10
}
```

**参数类：**

```java
@Data
public class BreakoutParam {
    private int breakoutPeriod = 20;        // 突破周期（N 日高点）
    private int volumeMaPeriod = 5;         // 均量周期
    private double volumeRatio = 2.0;       // 放量倍数
    private double breakoutMinPct = 0.01;   // 突破幅度过滤（%）
    private String stopMode = "KLINE_LOW";  // 止损方式：KLINE_LOW | ATR
    private int atrPeriod = 14;
    private double atrMultiple = 2.0;
    private double takeProfitRatio = 2.0;   // 盈亏比
    private int maxHoldingDays = 10;
}
```

**策略实现：**

```java
@Component
public class BreakoutStrategy implements QuantStrategy {

    private final StrategyParamService paramService;

    public BreakoutStrategy(StrategyParamService paramService) {
        this.paramService = paramService;
    }

    @Override
    public String getName() { return "breakout"; }

    @Override
    public ProductType getProductType() { return ProductType.STOCK; }

    @Override
    public StrategyResult evaluate(StockDataDTO data) {
        if (data == null || data.getKlineSeries() == null || data.getKlineSeries().size() < 2) {
            return StrategyResult.hold(ProductType.STOCK, data == null ? null : data.getSymbol());
        }
        BreakoutParam p = paramService.loadParam("breakout", BreakoutParam.class);
        List<KlineDTO> ks = data.getKlineSeries();
        KlineDTO today = ks.get(ks.size() - 1);

        // 1. 阻力位 = 前 N 日最高价（不含今日）
        BigDecimal resistance = TechnicalIndicator.resistanceHigh(ks, p.getBreakoutPeriod());
        if (resistance == null || resistance.signum() <= 0) {
            return StrategyResult.hold(ProductType.STOCK, data.getSymbol());
        }
        // 2. 放量确认
        double avgVol = TechnicalIndicator.avgVolume(ks, p.getVolumeMaPeriod());
        boolean volumeUp = today.getVolume() >= avgVol * p.getVolumeRatio();
        // 3. 收盘价突破
        boolean breakout = today.getClose().compareTo(resistance) > 0;
        // 4. 突破幅度过滤（过滤假突破）
        BigDecimal pct = today.getClose().subtract(resistance).divide(resistance, 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
        boolean enough = pct.compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(p.getBreakoutMinPct())) >= 0;

        if (!(breakout && volumeUp && enough)) {
            return StrategyResult.hold(ProductType.STOCK, data.getSymbol());
        }

        BigDecimal stopLoss = calcStopLoss(today, ks, p);
        BigDecimal target = today.getClose()
                .add(today.getClose().subtract(stopLoss).multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(p.getTakeProfitRatio())));

        return StrategyResult.builder()
                .productType(ProductType.STOCK).symbol(data.getSymbol())
                .signal("BUY").confidence(0.8)
                .entryPrice(today.getClose()).stopLoss(stopLoss).takeProfit(target)
                .reason(String.format("放量突破%d日高点, 量比%.2f", p.getBreakoutPeriod(), today.getVolume() / avgVol))
                .build();
    }

    private BigDecimal calcStopLoss(KlineDTO today, List<KlineDTO> ks, BreakoutParam p) {
        if ("ATR".equals(p.getStopMode())) {
            BigDecimal atr = TechnicalIndicator.atr(ks, p.getAtrPeriod());
            return today.getClose().subtract(atr.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(p.getAtrMultiple())));
        }
        return today.getLow();   // 默认：突破 K 线最低点
    }
}
```

### 6.14.3 缠论策略（分型 / 笔 / 中枢骨架）

> 缠论实现分 4 层自底向上：**① 分型 → ② 笔 → ③ 中枢 → ④ 买卖点**。V23 给出分型（含包含处理接口）+ 笔 + 中枢的骨架实现，买卖点（含 MACD 背驰）留 TODO 后期补充。

**值对象：**

```java
public enum FractalType { TOP, BOTTOM }

@Data @Builder
public class Fractal {
    private FractalType type;
    private int index;          // 分型 K 线在序列中的索引
    private BigDecimal price;   // 顶=最高价，底=最低价
}

@Data @Builder
public class Bi {
    private Fractal start;
    private Fractal end;
    private int startIndex;
    private int endIndex;

    public boolean isUp() { return start.getType() == FractalType.BOTTOM && end.getType() == FractalType.TOP; }
    public boolean isDown() { return start.getType() == FractalType.TOP && end.getType() == FractalType.BOTTOM; }
    public BigDecimal topPrice() { return start.getPrice().max(end.getPrice()); }
    public BigDecimal bottomPrice() { return start.getPrice().min(end.getPrice()); }
}

@Data @Builder
public class Zhongshu {
    private BigDecimal zg;      // 中枢上沿 = min(前三笔高点)
    private BigDecimal zd;      // 中枢下沿 = max(前三笔低点)
    private BigDecimal gg;      // 中枢最高
    private BigDecimal dd;      // 中枢最低
    private int startIndex;
    private int endIndex;
}
```

**缠论分析器（骨架）：**

```java
public class ChanlunAnalyzer {

    /** ① 分型识别（含 K 线包含关系合并） */
    public List<Fractal> detectFractals(List<KlineDTO> ks) {
        List<KlineDTO> merged = mergeInclusion(ks);
        List<Fractal> fractals = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 1; i < merged.size() - 1; i++) {
            BigDecimal high = merged.get(i).getHigh();
            BigDecimal low = merged.get(i).getLow();
            boolean top = high.compareTo(merged.get(i - 1).getHigh()) > 0
                    && high.compareTo(merged.get(i + 1).getHigh()) > 0;
            boolean bottom = low.compareTo(merged.get(i - 1).getLow()) < 0
                    && low.compareTo(merged.get(i + 1).getLow()) < 0;
            if (top) fractals.add(Fractal.builder().type(FractalType.TOP).index(i).price(high).build());
            else if (bottom) fractals.add(Fractal.builder().type(FractalType.BOTTOM).index(i).price(low).build());
        }
        return fractals;
    }

    /** K 线包含关系合并（缠论标准预处理，趋势向上取高高/低高，向下取低低/高低） */
    private List<KlineDTO> mergeInclusion(List<KlineDTO> ks) {
        // TODO: 完整包含处理——此处保留接口，落地时按趋势方向逐根合并
        return new ArrayList<>(ks);
    }

    /** ② 笔划分：顶底交替连接（骨架，完整版需加最少 K 线数过滤） */
    public List<Bi> detectBi(List<Fractal> fractals) {
        List<Bi> bis = new ArrayList<>();
        if (fractals.size() < 2) return bis;
        Fractal start = fractals.get(0);
        for (int i = 1; i < fractals.size(); i++) {
            Fractal end = fractals.get(i);
            if (start.getType() != end.getType()) {   // 顶底交替才成笔
                bis.add(Bi.builder().start(start).end(end)
                        .startIndex(start.getIndex()).endIndex(end.getIndex()).build());
                start = end;
            }
        }
        return bis;
    }

    /** ③ 中枢识别：连续 3 笔重叠区间（ZG=min 高点, ZD=max 低点） */
    public List<Zhongshu> detectZhongshu(List<Bi> bis) {
        List<Zhongshu> zs = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i + 2 < bis.size(); i++) {
            Bi b1 = bis.get(i), b2 = bis.get(i + 1), b3 = bis.get(i + 2);
            BigDecimal high = b1.topPrice().min(b2.topPrice()).min(b3.topPrice());
            BigDecimal low = b1.bottomPrice().max(b2.bottomPrice()).max(b3.bottomPrice());
            if (high.compareTo(low) > 0) {   // 有重叠区间
                zs.add(Zhongshu.builder().zg(high).zd(low)
                        .gg(maxTop(b1, b2, b3)).dd(minBottom(b1, b2, b3))
                        .startIndex(b1.getStartIndex()).endIndex(b3.getEndIndex()).build());
            }
        }
        return zs;
    }

    /** ④ 买卖点（骨架，后期补充：一买/二买/三买 + MACD 背驰） */
    public ChanlunSignal detectBuyPoints(List<Fractal> fractals, List<Zhongshu> zs) {
        // TODO: 一买=下跌末中枢后底分型+背驰；二买=一买后回踩不破前低；三买=离开中枢后回踩不进中枢
        return null;
    }

    private BigDecimal maxTop(Bi a, Bi b, Bi c) { return a.topPrice().max(b.topPrice()).max(c.topPrice()); }
    private BigDecimal minBottom(Bi a, Bi b, Bi c) { return a.bottomPrice().min(b.bottomPrice()).min(c.bottomPrice()); }
}
```

> **落地顺序**：第一阶段只做分型 + 笔 + 中枢（纯形态）；第二阶段加一买/二买（简化 MACD 背驰）；三买与精确背驰后期再调。

## 6.15 基本面因子计算（V23 新增）

### 6.15.1 公式表（对应 `qf_financial_factor` 字段）

| 因子 | 公式 | Tushare 字段 |
|:---|:---|:---|
| ROE | 归母净利润 / 归母净资产 | income 
_income_attr_p` ÷ balancesheet `total_hldr_eqy_exc_min_int` |
| ROA | 净利润 / 总资产 | income 
_income` ÷ balancesheet `total_assets` |
| 毛利率 | (营业收入 − 营业成本) / 营业收入 | income `revenue`、`total_cogs` |
| 净利率 | 净利润 / 营业收入 | income 
_income` ÷ `revenue` |
| 营收同比 | (本期营收 − 上年同期) / \|上年同期\| | income `revenue`（跨期） |
| 净利润同比 | (本期净利 − 上年同期) / \|上年同期\| | income 
_income`（跨期） |
| 资产负债率 | 总负债 / 总资产 | balancesheet `total_liab` ÷ `total_assets` |
| 流动比率 | 流动资产 / 流动负债 | balancesheet `total_cur_assets` ÷ `total_cur_liab` |
| 自由现金流 | 经营现金流净额 − 资本开支 | cashflow 
_cashflow_act` − `c_pay_acq_const_fiolta` |
| OCF/净利润 | 经营现金流净额 / 净利润 | cashflow 
_cashflow_act` ÷ income 
_income` |
| PE(TTM) | 总市值 / 近12月归母净利润 | daily_basic `pe_ttm`（直接取） |
| PB | 总市值 / 归母净资产 | daily_basic `pb`（直接取） |
| 应收账款/营收 | 应收账款 / 营业收入 | balancesheet `accounts_receiv` ÷ income `revenue` |
| 商誉/净资产 | 商誉 / 归母净资产 | balancesheet `goodwill` ÷ `total_hldr_eqy_exc_min_int` |

### 6.15.2 完整实现

```java
@Service
public class FundamentalFactorCalculator {

    /** 计算基本面因子，输出对应 qf_financial_factor 字段 */
    public FundamentalFactors calculate(String symbol, FinancialStatementsDTO stmt) {
        IncomeStatementDTO inc = stmt.getIncomeStatement();
        BalanceSheetDTO bal = stmt.getBalanceSheet();
        CashFlowDTO cf = stmt.getCashFlowStatement();
        return FundamentalFactors.builder()
                .symbol(symbol)
                .roe(div(inc.getNIncomeAttrP(), bal.getTotalHldrEqyExcMinInt()))
                .roa(div(inc.getNIncome(), bal.getTotalAssets()))
                .grossMargin(div(inc.getRevenue().subtract(inc.getTotalCogs()), inc.getRevenue()))
                .netMargin(div(inc.getNIncome(), inc.getRevenue()))
                .debtRatio(div(bal.getTotalLiab(), bal.getTotalAssets()))
                .currentRatio(div(bal.getTotalCurAssets(), bal.getTotalCurLiab()))
                .freeCashFlow(cf.getNCashflowAct() == null ? null : cf.getNCashflowAct().subtract(cf.getCPayAcqConstFiolta()))
                .ocfToNetProfit(div(cf.getNCashflowAct(), inc.getNIncome()))
                .arToRevenue(div(bal.getAccountsReceiv(), inc.getRevenue()))
                .goodwillToEquity(div(bal.getGoodwill(), bal.getTotalHldrEqyExcMinInt()))
                .build();
    }

    /** 除法 + null/除零保护 */
    private BigDecimal div(BigDecimal a, BigDecimal b) {
        return (a == null || b == null || b.signum() == 0) ? null
                : a.divide(b, 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
    }
}
```

> **注意**：① 同比（revenue_yoy / net_profit_yoy）需**同报表类型跨期**对比（Q1 对 Q1、年报对年报），非环比；② PE(TTM)/PB 直接用 `daily_basic` 现成值（Tushare 已算好 TTM），比自己拼 TTM 净利润可靠；③ 因子计算幂等：`(symbol, report_period, calc_date)` 唯一键 + UPSERT（§4.4）。

## 7. 数据分层存储架构

```
第1层 基础信息（MySQL）     qm_stock_basic / qs_strategy_config / qs_broker_config
第2层 行情数据（DuckDB/TSDB） qs_kline_bar / qs_order_book / qs_tick_data
第3层 业务数据（MySQL）      qt_trade_order / qt_trade_signal / qt_position / qt_trade_record
第4层 分析数据（MySQL）      qn_news_* / qf_financial_* / qs_backtest_* / qs_stock_pool*
缓存层（Redis）              最新盘口 / 最新 K 线 / 最新 Tick / 持仓快照 / 基础信息缓存(Caffeine)
```

**数据同步策略：**

| 数据类型 | 方向 | 频率 | 方式 |
|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| 基础信息 | 数据源 → MySQL | 日级 | XXL-JOB |
| K 线 | 数据源 → MySQL/DuckDB | 分钟~日级 | 增量（bar_time 去重） |
| 盘口 | QMT/CTP → Redis → DuckDB | 秒级 | 实时写 Redis，异步落盘 |
| Tick | QMT/CTP → Redis → DuckDB | 毫秒级 | 实时写 Redis，批量落盘 |
| 持仓 | 经纪商 → MySQL | 秒级(盘中)/日级(盘后) | 接口同步 |

**查询路由（`MarketDataQueryRouter`）：**

| 查询 | 路由 |
|:---|:---|
| 最近 N 根 K 线 | Redis |
| 历史 K 线 | DuckDB/TimescaleDB |
| 最新盘口/Tick | Redis |
| 历史盘口/Tick | DuckDB/TimescaleDB |
| 基础信息 | MySQL（可 Caffeine 缓存） |

**废弃表迁移映射（`qm_market_data` → 专用表）：**

| 原 data_type | 替代表 |
|:---|:---|
| KLINE | qs_kline_bar |
| ORDER_BOOK | qs_order_book |
| TICK | qs_tick_data |
| TRADE | qt_trade_record |
